Emmanuel; Chief Elect; our Example, stands boldly out in the character and life of the sanctified Christian (Acts 4:13,31); as would be observed through the sealbreak in the front-cover of the F. R., were the second and third wings eliminated (do not mar the F. R. by other, than mentally, "cutting-away" these wings). The mount of Transfiguration represents "all of self" -a full-consecration or total-abandonment to Godwhen, upon the sixth-day of character-creation, we have wholly overcome it or put it under-our-feet, we discover the "means by which the ascent was made:"-the development of Christ's spiritual mind—an inner, personal-fulfillment of the law and prophets (Rev.10:7; 22:6).

But after the conquest-our task is "but, just begun" -we must descend from this rarified-spiritual atmosphere, to share our enlightenment with a darkened, groaning world below; pointing out to others the trail which leads to the top; describing to them-with the faulty language, at our commend-the "glorious-prospect of their better-selves," which awaits them at the summit. Where they will find, that the mystery is turned into revelation by "spiritualizing the books of the Law and prophets" (Moses and Elijah were present in spirit). They all teach us how Christ, our hope of glory, may be formed within. And the promise of his second-appearing is thus, conditionally fulfilled (Heb. 

9:28; 1 Jhn.4:17). All the mystery of character or image building is solved by breaking the seventh-seal, which reveals a fully-matured mind (1 Cor.2:7-16; Rev. 

5:6), and perfect (preferences, 1 Jhn.4:3-4,12-19) love. 

Judgment (the sixth) day has come (Gen.1:31; Rev. 

6:12-17). The photographer proffers his Customer the portrait for which he posed (Eph. 4:13; 1 Pet. 2:21).

After scrutiny . . . it is accepted with "unconcealed pleasure" (Heb.13:21; 2 Pet.1:17-19; Rev.1:17). The photographer feels highly complimented in having "acceptably served" his most-reverend Master. And-behold! Mystery of mysteries-the finished-image is now, moreover, a "perfect likeness" of the photographer himself (1 Jhn.3:2,3,7; 4:17). Who has, via contact, contracted the "spirit of the Word" (Jhn.4:23-24; 6:63)-

while obediently engaged in the "King's business" (Lk. 

2:49; 6:40).

As indicated by the rear of the fifth-wing of the F. R., the perfected-Christian sees himself "as he is seen," when the finished-portrait is held upside-down for the King's approval, or via explanation. The upper-wheel or enlightening-testimony thus comes to earth (Eze.1: 

15), viz., God's image and likeness is "so revealed upon the sixth-day of character-creation" (Gen.1:24-27; Rom. 8:29).

Inside the sixth-wing of the F. R., over against the positive-light of the time-of-the-end, we see the negative leviathan-lamp or experience-of-fear typed, which is the beginning of (wisdom, Psa.111:10; Mat.23:12) the end. 

And above all, the promises-of-God are the most exalted or highly-prized part of the Christian's-heritage (Jos. 

21:43:45; 1 Ki.8:56), unlocking every sealed-mystery (Eph.3:3-6; Rev.10:7). 

On the backside of the F. R. the "entire-science of spiritual-photography"- Wisdom's plan of character, soul, or image building is portrayed (Jhn.8:31-32; 

Heb.8:10). The "negative carnal-mind," representing the first-installment of salvation, obtained under the law by the foolish-virgin, is here contrasted with the "positive spiritual-mind" of the wise-virgin, obtained under grace, as second-installment of holiness.

These comparisons can only be made in the light of an Eveready lamp or revelation (Eze.1:13; Lk.17:24; 

1 Cor.2:13-16; 1 Pet.3:15). No wise judge will call for a leviathan or negative witness, who can explain but one-half of the mystery-of-godliness (Mat.25:8; Heb.5: 

10-14). He will certainly seek out a positive, all-theway witness-with an experience or lamp-of-knowledge, which makes him Eveready with a "reasonable-answer for the hope within him," that he is a component part of Christ's-Bride (Rev.19:7-8).

This diagram, on the backside of the F. R., types a review of the Finished Mystery (Col.1:24-29; Rev.10: 

7). The carnal-mind can now, only be represented by a red image-of-the-devil or personified-duality. And the ashamed spiritual-mind must be typed by a dark imageof-Christ, for this is the analytic-judgment of the perfect-saint, who breaks the sixth-seal of character-formation. Who also, as a master-builder, naturally considers

BROKEN "BANDS" OR MYSTERY-Seals his mansion from a "constructive standpoint" (1 Cor. 

3:9-12; Eph.2:19-22). While an admirer estimates its value from a viewpoint of general-utility only, or "what it would be worth to him," were he the owner-not what it cost the other-fellow, who built it. 

The photographer, in making portraits, has the "whole-science of his profession" in mind, when he sets a valuation upon his services. While his customers can see, nothing but a fascinating-image. We hope that this analysis of "mystery portraits" will make them appreciated! 

This "symbol of God's law" agrees with personal experience, that Christian-character cannot be developed without a dual-nature (Gen.3:15; Psa.109:6; Zech. 

3:1; Jas.1:2-5, 12-17). Reason insists that there must be a contrast-negative evil vs. positive good-in the mind (Deu.30:15,19; 32:29-30; Isa.27:1; 45:7; Amo.3:6; 

Rom.5:3-5; 7:21; 8:7; Gal.5:17; Eph.5:13-17), like a dual electric-lamp, or there will not be any light or knowledge-of-the-truth resulting. 

A controversy, cross, or indecision exists in the mind, as a "result of this contrast," which is emblemized in the center of the diagram-directly opposite to the outer faith - piercing, inner mercy - extension breach of the front-cover, indicative of the fact that the sum-total of divine-law discipline is "Christian cross-bearing” (Lk. 

9:23; 14:25-33).

The first-born and the old-man must die in the ordeal, for they are the alternate crossing-leviathan bars of God's law, twice made perpendicular in the double X 

XX 12X of the XXXXXXX, in a conformity to his will and At-one-ment (Psa.11:67,71; Rom.6:3-13; 1 Cor.15: 

42-54; Eph.4:22-27; Col.3:3-10).

Man's spirit, upon the sixth-day of character-creation, as indicated by the black-profile, mourns the fact that it is clothed with the "forced-to-wear garment of physical-lust or negative-fabrication" (Deu.33:29; Rom.7:24; 

1 Cor.10:20-21; Rev.6:12)—just as the photographer must acknowledge that his truth-telling portraits exist, by virtue of the fact that he first created a foundationof-lies. The moon or reflecting-mind of the Christian, also becomes subservient to the dictation of the sun-like spirit, resident in the blood (Gen.9:4; Acts 2:20)—just as the photographer's negative must give place to the positive. The light-of-truth always reverses a dark lie-

in a clean spiritual-mind and thus character is transformed. And when the stars or characteristics-of-godliness (Gen.22:17; Dan.12:3; Rev.12:1), fall out of the heavenly-mind into the body-of-earth, then there is a new earth-or-nature (Rev.6:13; 21:1): a resurrection.

The symbolic-language of Isa.30:26, which is descrip tive of the Finished Mystery, reads: Moreover the Light of the Moon shall be as the Light of the Sun [the moon types the dependent reflecting-mind. The sun emblemizes the conscientious - spirit of the Christian. As the moon borrows its light from the sun, so does the humanmind reflect by virtue of the spirit's presence in the body (Jas.2:26). The physical-dead know nothing; but the universal-spirit, when it leaves the body, returns to 

God who gave it (Ecl.12:7); and may, D. V., inhabit other sun-illuminated planets, which have one or more moons of their own. When the mind is spiritualized, reason agrees with conscience to overcome bestial-sense. 

Also, when man's will and God's stand perpendicular (Isa.28:17), the cross-of-Christianity ceases to exist, Eph.2:12-18; Rev.18:23.], and the Light of the Sun shall be Sevenfold [spiritual-perfection is the result of sevenfold-experience] as the Light of Seven Days [the seven days or revelations of the "week of charactercreation" enlighten us with a knowledge of the mysteryof-godliness, 1 Tim.3:16; 4:8], in the Day that the Lord

Bindeth up the Breach of his People [the great gulf, gap, breach, or difference-in-character, between Christ and the sinner, is closed after the sinner's rebirth and spiritual-maturity, Lk.16:26], and Healeth the Stroke of their Wound [the sword-of-the-Spirit or the word-of- God wounds or condemns the conscience of the transgressor (Eze.21:9-11; 29:6,7; Heb.4:12), and this condemnation All disappears with the Two Witnesses or double-sealing of the Spirit, 1 Jhn.3:21; 4:18; 5:7-8].

As observed by the second and third wings of the F. R., which represent the two plates of image-making, leviathan-overcoming begins on the rear of the negative, and extends over on the front-side of the positive plate, like a "continued story." Moreover, the temple measurements and comparisons (2 Cor.6:16; 13:5), begin on the rear of the positive-plate; and extend way back to the top-most front of the negative. This arrangement symbolizes the fact that, the two experiences of regeneration are closely interlocked or dovetailed together:

each being indispensable to the other. Justification brings the Christian up to the very border of the Promised Land experience, with his leviathan-lamp (Mat. 

25:7,8); but he cannot possibly go further. On the other hand, the sanctified-experience is typed by Canaan, which looked to Egypt to give birth to a "nation-ofvirtues," to drive out the wicked nations-or-propensities which disgraced its soil (Rom. 8:21-23). Solomon's

Temple also typically referred to, or was reliant upon, the "nethermost layer-of-stones" comprising its foundation; it, too, housed all of the Tabernacle features and furniture, of the past.

One may readily judge, by the prominence alloted to it upon the walls of memory-the comparative second and third wings of the F. R.-how expedient it is to bear in mind the descriptive leviathan-overcoming, which answers as a "substitute for many portraits." Also, the word speaks emphatically regarding the significance of comparative temple-typology (Eze.43:10-12; 44:5). 

Moreover, the blackness of God-sent sorrow, fear, and shame of imperfection, should be considered an essential one-half part of character-building (Psa.119:67,71; Heb. 

12:6-13). Only one side of these-wings are black: there is a "hidden record" and an "exposed or backside" to every investigated character, the "mental-anguish or crucifixion," and the "pleasing after-effect" (Rom.8:18; 

2 Cor.4:16-17). Both records should be carefully folded away, or kept in mind for Eveready reference. The sublime-aspect of the day-revelation of God's purpose; and the depressing-trials of the night-unpleasant crossbearing-are both of "equal value, in planning and explaining character-building." 

When the F. R. is unfolded, the "leviathan-overcoming" lays alongside the "dark-fear of the sinner" and "shame of the foolish-virgin." The leviathan physicallife, or law-working in their members, and governing their minds-is what ails them both. All this reads upside-down, in connection with the body-or-cover of the F. R.-bottom or wrong-side up. Down is the direction, in which the sinner generally, and the foolishvirgin often, are prone to go. Their rebellious and carnal minds are full of darkness or ignorance-of-the-mystery; causing them to slip and stumble (Pro.4:19; Rom. 1:21).

The four upper-wings of the F. R. symbolize the regression of their minds and spirits. The covers of the 

F. R., typing their bodies, read right-side up and-judging from all pretenses and external appearances there is "nothing-at-all amiss on the inside." Neither the past nor the future seems at all to disturb them (Psa.73:27). But the unfolded F. R. reveals the light in which God sees them; and in which they must confess themselvesbefore they can be delivered from their "awful predicament," 

Moreover, the second and third wings-typing the comparative-knowledge of the gospel-enlightened mind -are attached to the front-cover of the F. R., which represents the past. While the fourth and fifth wings, typing the spirit of man — which is condemned and judged, according to the light received in the mind, in the past is cut off from the body, in the future; which is symbolized by the rear-cover of the F. R. ...

There is an eternal heaven and hell contrast between overcoming the F. R. and its overcoming you; just as definite as its rear-cover retrospect reverses its frontcover prospect. A bird's-eye view of this folder-when closed-conceals the distressing sacrifice-altering Forecourt, and the self-denying Rearcourt-with their connecting (Jerk-at-whoa) Jericho road. The two holies and the Ark's dark-interior are open and accessible, as a possession; being "basic factors" of the saintly heaven where sadness is excluded and gladness included. 

To the contrary, an upward-look at the F. R. Forecourt painfully recalls life's shirked-sacrifices, and the spurned-services of the fourfold-saints of its Rearcourt.

The Most Holy positive-blessing of Christ's "imparted at-one-ment or righteousness is exposed tantalizingly; but its preceding negative key-blessing of imputed-holiness, and the fundamental image developing and stabilizing dark-room, are closed doors of mercy and opportunity. These fixed laws of destiny seal the sinner's doom and unavoidably create a remorseful hell-in the second or too-late resurrection of damnation (Jhn.5: 29).

The F. R. from above or below-types the "wedge of faith and persistence" by which a Christian pilgrim elbows and shoulders his way forward to progress against an ever-opposing worldly environment.-Mat. 7:14.

Is it not a "truly-wonderful advantage and satisfaction," for a man to be able to locate himself; and to "watchfully" discover what he really is; and to know just "how much farther he must progress, spiritually, before he can enter into his rest?" All of this truth is revealed in the light of the Finished Mystery; and witnessed to by the F. R. But-better still-experienced by All personified Eveready-flashlights!-Eze.1:13-28. 

The treasure which Everybody desires, needs, and unconsciously seeks-little knowing their own minds-is the F. R. revealed-mystery of Christ's second-coming. It is well said, that "the Creator has wisely reserved a place, for himself-alone, in our hearts."

Matrimony is the mistaken, unthinking, will-o'-thewisp "forever-afterward-happy conclusion" of most fiction-stories, and of practically every motion-picture exhibited for the modern-public's consumption. Nevertheless, precious scriptural-symbolism is there! A mating with Christ-the Bridegroom of our souls-is the only sensible end-of-life. The bliss of "that union" affords a substantial, consistent-by every test—satisfaction, which can't possibly disappoint us in time or eternity. 

We can forget our worries while at work or play; but haunting-conviction betimes demands an honest-solution of life's-problem: Who, what, where am I? Where did I come from? Whence shall I go from here? Why exist-am I making good? 

To decide intelligently, we suggest that you beware all antichrist "self-pitying, deception, and indulgence tactics"-as you would mislabeled poison-quit procrastinating and, while you may, finish your world-mission; oblivious of all opposition to your personal-interests, knowing that nothing is so sure as death, nor as uncertain as life . . . in reckoning with opportunity. Once this "all-important matter" is settled, you can do the future justice.

Finally, are you the epistle of Christ, known and read of all men? If so, congratulate yourself! If not, be wise -continue in the portrait business-until Your Future is assured! for This lecture contains a Formula Christian Worship - working out 

All the beautiful-features of Christ's second-coming! -Rom.8:28-30; Phil.2:5-13.

All lost sinners-mortals of fate-are naturally outside the front F. R. cover; and all perfect saints are, in contrast, beyond the rear cover. like this, can possibly be the visibilized-body of the original visualized, mystic or spiritual F. R. 

Strait is the gate, and narrow the way, which leadeth unto life, and few there be that find it (Mat.7:14).

But if you fail to make any marked improvements, then why not share liberally in the writer's joys, and the benefits of his discoveries?

T. principle of "worshiping by proxy," gave way before the N. T. "direct-approach to God." Israel's Wilderness-march is the interesting

Bible-portrayal of the first heaven-out of slavery, into freedom. Israel's battles for the possession of his inheritance that the Promised-land might become Holyland, is the exciting second heaven. The retrospective 

Temple building, and peaceful king Solomon's reign, types the third heaven-minus wars and wandering. This is the most heavenly, for it includes both of the former. 

Why should bigots resent this explanation of the heavens; would they keep them forever concealed? Experience will change their minds. . . if they think Such too easy of access!

With leviathan, the sea of temptation-in which he swims-and the two ships or winged witnesses-of-transport overcome, the blackness behind the F. R. throne no longer represents fear, sorrow, shame, and condemnation-but the negative-half of instructive-contrast, developing Wisdom.

To the rear, or out of these dark-pages, the four looking-up or spiritual creatures are symbolized by their burning lamps-of-testimony. This blackness, of their past, warns them to beware of the subtlety of sin and carnality. Their Jonah-in-leviathan experience is ever opposite, before their faces, stimulating their faithfulness; and answering the arguments of blind critics and persecutors.

Only the middle-pages of the F. R. are like those of ordinary books. Here you see, to the left, a diagram of the house of Solomon's Temple, exposing to view the Porch, Holy, and Most Holy, in comparison with the similarly constructed Eveready-leviathan flashlight, and the prostrate-bodies of old and new dispensational worshipers-of-God. 

To the right you see the typification of four beasts; four and twenty elders; and judgment's - fireworks of thunder, lightning, and wrathful voices the Elects' emblematic overcoming, of that below. All of law and 

---

prophecy is strikingly contrasted in Old and New Testament types, in the foursquare, cube-like Most Holy of the F. R. The electric-flashlight makes these comparisons essentially a time-of-the-end revelation (Dan.12: 4,9).

Nothing more of the inner F. R. is directly accessible, unless we "pull-down the curtain or fourth - wing." 

Along with it comes the third-wing, typing a Most Holy change-of-mind; making readable the last-half of leviathan-overcoming, without which the first-half is sadly disjointed.

There is but one standard-of-godliness for everybody; and God's treasury-of-holiness, in the F. R. manipulation, is thrown wide open for general inspection. Wisdom must decree the degree of Christlikeness which we personally accept; but the F. R. offers God's-best first. 

His second-best is hidden from view, and reserved for backward spiritual-babes, and foolish-virgins. 

The F. R. hell and heaven appear upon opposite sides of its spirit-wings. Their origin is explained on the two corresponding mind-wings at the left-which contrasts in the first case, and confirms in the second. 

The news of "leviathan being-led-captive," as a twice witnessing flash-of-light in the night, feeds the fire of the sinner's hell, and day (revelation) of the Lord of the dual-Christian. What a mortal has unrevokably done, or what he helplessly is, don't condemn him likethe knowledge of what he can and should be. Benighted associations, bad habits, and past sins of ommission and commission, do cause concern. But the fact that he is now a willing or unwilling slave to his flesh or leviathan-life, and no better in that respect, than the lower-species of dumb-brutes, is indeed very embarressing; and a "resented accusation."

Justice more effectually condemns a man with good, than with bad, or by what he isn't rather than by what he is. That's why the future leviathan-conquest is outlined first to the left in the F. R.; and the fear, sorrow, and shame of bondage is placed second, to the right. 

Orderly, the way out is to humbly settle the old-account, or face about in repentance, at the bottom or "conclusion of all wrong-doing." . It will be noticed that the "origin of heaven" is past with the Christian. His body has been made Holy and 

Most Holy, like the house of Solomon's Temple; and he

has the flashlight and ship-like witnesses to both installments of holiness or heaven. 

To be "watchfully" sure that one is Christlike or has the antitype-of-holiness, the central mind-cube of the sanctuary must reversely divorce the flesh and annex itself to the spirit-cube. This being mentally absent from the body and present with the Lord, exchanges O. T. Levitical priesthood for N. T. Judaistic priesthood (Heb.7:9-17).

On page-ten, of the folded F. R., we see that the Eveready-lamp of Christian-testimony, which was uppermost and future at conversion, when the leviathan nethermost-lamp, of the unfolded F. R., was being figuratively kindled, now has the floor; and is witnessing or imparting light to its justified, consecrated, and sanctified associates, as the perfect-Example on page-seven. Every "watchful" sacrifice tends to spiritual-growth and fourfold-unfolding, until we are not the least, but among the greatest of the kingdom's spiritual-creatures. Nothing but a progressive-manipulation of the witnessing F. R. reverses the position of these lamps, from top to bottom; from left to right; and vice versa.

Necessarily, the folded F. R. denotes concluding-rest, which was primarily the goal-of-incentive. Leviathan aflame, as portrayed inside the rear-cover, is ever the foundation of Wisdom or character-building. Its superstructure appears outside, as an advanced time-of-theend experience. The inner-lower and outer-upper blend together, even as the printer's ink is absorbed by the paper from both sides.

As the first and sixth (body) wings, the second and third (mind) wings, and the fourth and fifth (spirit) wings, are inseparably tied together the triple-test of 

F. R. "watchfulness" is a pairing three ways.

Nothing but body, mind, and spirit progress will ever open the door, where a man's good, bétter, and best self awaits to welcome him with a glad "Well done, good and faithful servant." As we advance from one roomof-experience to another, the front-door is always the one ahead; and upon finishing character-building, every entered-door becomes rear. The withdrawal of the leviathan and insertion of an Eveready flashlight-battery, has all to do with the rear-end of these lamps. Reviewing faces us down (night) ward; and expectancy faces us up (light, Eph.2:1-22; 5:13) ward.

An Eveready-lamp is nowhere in sight for less than half-way pilgrims. Though every flashlight is originally a leviathan, and every Christian is at first a foolish virgin-yet it would be an injustice to this wonderfullamp, to even refer to it as a leviathan or single-battery specimen. Correspondingly, Christ warns us that we are in danger of hell-fire, if we call our (young, Mat. 

5:22) brethren fools.

The initial-cost of flashlights, in all but rare cases, exceeds the value of a one-battery service. The foolishvirgin's lamp is destined to flicker and go out. But the wise-virgin's lamp is a "genuine bargain," in that the trifling-cost of the second, and succeeding batteries, the installation of which justifies the name "Eveready for service," makes its upkeep inconsiderate, in comparison to the benefit received.

The name leviathan is perfectly proper for the mystic or questioning Job 41 flashlight-description. But the 

F. R. manipulation, like the light, is similar in analyzing either a wise or, foolish virgin's experience; for this lamp types a human-body to which the foolish-virgin originally belongs; but the wise-virgin makes it hers in overcoming. Both she and her lamp receive new names, when captivity is led captive . as Jacob's name became Is-real at Peniel. The F. R. "leviathan in-captivity" interpretation, alludes to the dormant possibilities of every human-body -when made servant, instead of master-via a covenanted change of governing mind and heart. Even as electricity is harnessed in the up-to-date household of the time-of-the-end, supplying light, heat, and power. 

The saints, who have thus led captivity captive, would no more consider giving unbridled-rein to their flesh, than recklessly disregarding the rules for using their electrical-appliances.

Every person is originally a Jonah-like prisoner in his own body, until similarly liberated via repentance.

Then he is wise "to the extent of profiting by sordidexperience" (Jna.2:6-7; Mat.12:39-41; Gal.3:23-27). 

Without light, one would not know the "need of house-cleaning," nor yet be able to locate the dirt. Spiritual-cleanliness is made possible through an introduction of added and multiplied Eveready-batteries, or mystic teachers, who constitute the third-person-or-officialdom of the trinity (Jhn.16:7-14; Heb.1:14; 13:2).

What man would purchase a flashlight, if he thought that it would only serve him during the life-time of a single battery? In all reason he expects to replace the first battery with a second, etc.-making possible a revelation of what would otherwise be hid, lost, or out of reach. 

Do you suppose that any intelligent flashlight-owner is disappointed, in the course of time, to discover that his light is growing dim, or perchance is totally extinguished; and is worthless in its dead-condition? He at first had no need of an additional or replacement battery; but now he must forego the use of his lamp, and be unprepared for emergencies; unless he pays the price for a fresh battery, or installment of "watchfulness" (Eph.4:22-24; 5:13-18). Is not this experience symbolical of "spiritual-foresightedness?"

Constant repetition-of-batteries is the covenant or original-understanding between the dealer and customer; disregard of which, releases the merchant and manufacturer from all obligations, in case of loss or accident. 

The "watching" for Christ's second-coming implies a manifold-comparison with, and effort to be like Him (Eph.4:13-15). As the Eveready-lamp has many batteries-or-installments of light-so do all the angels of God wait upon the-virgin or Eveready-Christian to impart some new-revelation of his or her better-self (Psa. 

91:11; 2 Thes.1:7).

God's law is not only represented by the dual-electricity of the flashlight-battery, but by the "scientific - adjustment of its negative and positive cells," so that they may be pitted against each other at will: thus producing light in the lamp of the head. A consideration of good and evil in the mind, produces an argument in the tongue, for or against every thought, word, and deed; as the science of harnessing manufactured-electricity characterizes the flashlight. This is all symbolical of the "wisdom that wills positive-spirit the master of negative-flesh❞—the mind being the arena of conflict or adjustment.

In explanation of the time-of-the-end diagram upon the rear F. R. cover: when the sun becomes black as sackcloth of hair, e.g., the spirit mourns its crudegarment of flesh; and the moon, mind of reflection, becomes as blood: is intuitively guided by the spirit, resi-

dent therein that is the "climax of Christian-overcoming." 

The fourfold-gospel of scripture treats of the four spiritual creatures-of-perfection. The first-pair is dualminded; the second-pair is single-minded. These are ever at variance, as their understanding and interests are different, just as children and adults disagree. But the younger must be made to obey their elders. Moonlike reflection or imitation distinguishes the first-pair. 

Sunlight originality distinguishes the second - pair. 

Christ and his adversary; divine and human nature; and positive vs. negative law, of the Christian cross, are here symbolized.

All justified Christians or Church members should (like eagles) be provident. All consecrated pastors should be (like oxen) ready for service or sacrifice. All sanctified evangelists should be (like bearded lions) fearless hunters of prey. All perfected bishops should be (Christlike) pillars of the Church; being fourfold experienced, they are examples to their subordinates. 

The two horns of the F. R. ox, represent the condensed commandments-one's duty to God and man (Mat.22:27-40). The O. T. pastoral-spirit was ox-like or dutiful. N. T. pastors are supposed to be calf-like pleaders or advocates: real-shepherds (compare Jer.3:15; Eze. 

1:10; Rev.4:7). Duty drives. Love constrains-it "dehorns the beast-of-servitude." Motives, as various as circumstances, are interchangeable. Self-denial is virtuous; but preference serves more gracefully. Duty bears a cross. Love is crossless.

The pastor (in the red) is the mind of his flock, being one with the lesser makes him negative. The bishop (in the black) is the mind of his evangelists, whose denunciation-of-sin should be as awe-inspiring as the roaring-of-a-lion, which is king-of-beasts. His manner should be bold, as he stalks the prey and wins souls. If filled with the Holy Spirit, he will have the lion's-disposition: never sparing the sheep-of-the-flock, who are at ease in 

Zion (indifferent as to Signs, Amos 6:1; Mat.16:3; 24: 30,42-43).

It is not only possible for individual-Christians, but for honest congregations and denominations to "profitably locate themselves behind some one or more of the F. R. mystery-walls." It is scripturally stated that Christian-assemblies are progressively one to seven degrees

apart. Moreover, what we call Babylon or Confusion-ofcreeds, is graduated and easily accounted for by these invisible schism-curtains, which wall some churches in and others out... anything but manifestations of brotherly-love.

We cannot remedially advise a united-apostasy, so we urge a "family-reunion in sevenfold spiritual-advancement," as a positive-guarantee for "consistent and perfect harmony" in Christ's second-coming body.

How many church-demonimnations, with their worldly compromise and antichrist - tactics, can reasonably claim to be in the spiritual-sixth Philadelphia schoolgrade? The time must come when the "fundamentalterm of HOLINESS," when applied to Christian churches, will not be derisive. . . but complimentarily orthodox. 

Church weakness and blindness has been largely due to the backward to-day worship of a yesterday-or-historical "Christ of its Ephesus-infancy," to the utter ignoring of "his mature Laodicean inner-reappearance.' 

Now, actually, he stands at the sealed front-door of his back-door Church, bidding for attention; and seeking practical admission, via a reproduction in the individual-members of his body, who will surrender their "inferiority complex" to make room for a "as he is, so are we in this world" equality (1 Jhn.4:17; Rev.3:20).

The above introduction-of-the-Word is the result of consulting him personally, in book-form, and experimentally. He and you will be either pleased or offended-perhaps doubly effected? Yet comforting is the hope of rendering a mutual service; thus Breaking 

Bread-profit sharing three ways (Mat.18:20). 

The end of this Lecture; but the F. R. suggests countless others, of equal value—as the subject of spiritual progress is inexhaustible.
